    12	// Snapshot rewrite throttle: journal append ALWAYS; the snapshot (and
    13	// HANDOFF.md) re-render only on an important event type, >30 s since the last
    14	// snapshot, or >=10 accreted events (plan §Checkpoint engine).
    15	const ROUTINE_TYPES = new Set(['note', 'file.touch']);
    16	const THROTTLE_WINDOW_MS = 30_000;
    17	const THROTTLE_EVENT_COUNT = 10;
    18	
    19	// Transcript tail bounds (plan §Transcript policy): last 10 messages, 8 KB cap,
    20	// opt-in via baton.config.json capture.transcriptTail, PreCompact only.
    21	const TAIL_MESSAGES = 10;
    22	const TAIL_BYTES = 8192;
    23	
    24	/**
    25	 * Opt-in transcript-tail capture (gate-2 fix 10). Returns the bounded,
    26	 * redacted tail or null when the gate is closed: config off (the default),
    27	 * not a PreCompact payload, no usable transcript_path, or an unreadable /
    28	 * symlinked file. The path comes from an untrusted hook payload — it is read
    29	 * only under explicit opt-in, must lstat as a regular file, and its content
    30	 * passes the secret-redaction filter before storage.
    31	 * @param {string} root @param {any} raw parsed hook payload @param {any} io
    32	 * @returns {string | null}
    33	 */
    34	function captureTranscriptTail(root, raw, io) {
    35	  const cfg = safeReadJson(io.fs, `${root}/baton.config.json`);
    36	  if (!cfg.ok || cfg.value?.capture?.transcriptTail !== true) return null;
    37	
    38	  const eventName = raw && typeof raw === 'object' ? (raw.hook_event_name ?? raw.event) : null;
    39	  if (typeof eventName !== 'string' || !/precompact/i.test(eventName)) return null;
    40	  const path = raw.transcript_path;
    41	  if (typeof path !== 'string' || path.length === 0) return null;
    42	
    43	  try {
    44	    if (!io.fs.lstatSync(path).isFile()) return null;
    45	    // Allowlist the transcript path (gate-2 iter-2 m1): the payload is
    46	    // untrusted, so its resolved realpath must sit under the repo root, the
    47	    // user's ~/.claude tree (where Claude Code actually writes transcripts),
    48	    // or a configured capture.transcriptDir — never an arbitrary readable file.
    49	    const fileReal = io.fs.realpathSync(path);
    50	    // On POSIX a backslash is a LEGAL filename character, not a separator, so
    51	    // normalizing '\\'→'/' in the containment check (isContained) could make an
    52	    // out-of-tree sibling like '/repo\\evil/f' appear contained (iter-4 I9).
    53	    // The transcript path is the one fully-untrusted path that reaches
    54	    // containment, so refuse a backslash-bearing realpath off Windows.
    55	    if (io.platform !== 'win32' && String(fileReal).includes('\\')) return null;
    56	    /** @type {string[]} */
    57	    const allowed = [root];
    58	    const home = io.env?.HOME;
    59	    if (typeof home === 'string' && home.length > 0) allowed.push(`${home}/.claude`);
    60	    const dir = cfg.value?.capture?.transcriptDir;
    61	    if (typeof dir === 'string' && dir.length > 0) allowed.push(dir);
    62	    const under = (/** @type {string} */ base) => {
    63	      let baseReal;
    64	      try {
    65	        baseReal = io.fs.realpathSync(base);
    66	      } catch {
    67	        baseReal = base;
    68	      }
    69	      // Compare on normalized forward-slash spellings (iter-3 F9): raw Windows
    70	      // backslash realpaths never satisfy a forward-slash containment, so
    71	      // opt-in capture would silently never fire.
    72	      return isContained(baseReal, fileReal);
    73	    };
    74	    if (!allowed.some(under)) return null;
    75	
    76	    const text = io.fs.readFileSync(path, 'utf8');
    77	    const lines = text.split('\n').filter((/** @type {string} */ l) => l.trim() !== '');
    78	    let tail = lines.slice(-TAIL_MESSAGES).join('\n');
    79	    if (Buffer.byteLength(tail) > TAIL_BYTES) {
    80	      tail = Buffer.from(tail).subarray(-TAIL_BYTES).toString('utf8');
    81	    }
    82	    return redactSecrets(tail);
    83	  } catch {
    84	    return null; // unreadable transcript is never a checkpoint failure
    85	  }
    86	}
    87	
    88	/**
    89	 * `baton checkpoint` — mechanical checkpoint from a hook payload on stdin.
    90	 * Hook-safety rule: exit 0 on every soft failure so a checkpoint can never
    91	 * break the host harness; `--strict` opts into hard failures (CI).
    92	 * @param {string[]} args @param {any} io
    93	 * @returns {Promise<number>}
    94	 */
    95	export async function cmdCheckpoint(args, io) {
    96	  const { flags } = parseFlags(args);
    97	  const strict = flags.strict === true;
    98	  const platform = typeof flags.platform === 'string' ? flags.platform : null;
    99	  if (!platform) return usageError(io, flags, 'checkpoint', '--platform <claude-code|codex|cursor> is required');
   100	
   101	  try {
   102	    return await run(flags, platform, io);
   103	  } catch (err) {
   104	    const msg = /** @type {any} */ (err)?.message ?? String(err);
   105	    if (flags.json) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: false, error: { code: 'checkpoint-failed', msg } });
   106	    io.stderr.write(`baton checkpoint: ${msg}\n`);
   107	    return strict ? 1 : 0;
   108	  }
   109	}
   110	
   111	/**
   112	 * @param {Record<string, string | boolean>} flags
   113	 * @param {string} platform
   114	 * @param {any} io
   115	 * @returns {Promise<number>}
   116	 */
   117	async function run(flags, platform, io) {
   118	  const strict = flags.strict === true;
   119	  const root = resolveRoot(io, flags);
   120	  const paths = bundlePaths(root);
   121	  // The --json contract (gate-2 fix 11): exactly one envelope on stdout for
   122	  // every exit path — the exit code stays governed by hook-safety.
   123	  const finish = (/** @type {any} */ env, /** @type {number} */ code) => {
   124	    if (flags.json) emitEnvelope(io, env);
   125	    return code;
   126	  };
   127	
   128	  /** @type {any} */
   129	  let raw;
   130	  try {
   131	    raw = JSON.parse(typeof io.stdin === 'string' ? io.stdin : '');
   132	  } catch {
   133	    io.stderr.write('baton checkpoint: stdin could not be parsed as JSON — input ignored\n');
   134	    return finish({ ok: false, error: { code: 'bad-stdin', msg: 'stdin could not be parsed as JSON' } }, strict ? 1 : 0);
   135	  }
   136	
   137	  const session = { host: io.host, pid: io.pid, startTime: io.startTime };
   138	  const events = normalizeHookPayload(raw, platform, session);
   139	  if (events.length === 0) return finish({ ok: true, data: { events: 0, rewritten: false } }, 0);
   140	
   141	  // Opt-in transcript tail (gate-2 fix 10): rides the journal as its own
   142	  // IMPORTANT event so the snapshot rewrite below persists it, and purge can
   143	  // strip it from every retained copy by its property name.
   144	  const tail = captureTranscriptTail(root, raw, io);
   145	  if (tail !== null) {
   146	    events.push({
   147	      type: 'transcript.set',
   148	      payload: { transcript: { capturedAt: io.now(), tail } },
   149	      source: platform,
   150	      sessionHint: events[0]?.sessionHint,
   151	      unstable: events[0]?.unstable ?? true,
   152	    });
   153	  }
   154	
   155	  const { bundle, warnings, unsafe } = loadBundle(root, io);
   156	  for (const w of warnings) io.stderr.write(`baton checkpoint: ${w}\n`);
   157	
   158	  // An unsafe managed tree (symlinked .handoff — gate-2 fix 6) is NOT "no
   159	  // bundle yet": seeding here would write through the link. Refuse everything;
   160	  // hook-safety keeps the refusal soft outside --strict.
   161	  if (unsafe === true) {
   162	    io.stderr.write('baton checkpoint: managed tree failed the symlink/realpath jail — nothing written\n');
   163	    return finish({ ok: false, error: { code: 'unsafe-tree', msg: warnings[0] ?? 'managed tree failed the jail check' } }, strict ? 1 : 0);
   164	  }
   165	
   166	  // Opt-in debounce (gate-2 major 14; Cursor afterFileEdit fires per edit):
   167	  // at most one real checkpoint per window, tracked by a per-platform stamp.
   168	  // The read-and-set runs UNDER the repo lock (gate-2 iter-2 M5) so two
   169	  // simultaneous afterFileEdit hooks can't both observe no stamp and both
   170	  // checkpoint — exactly one wins the window.
   171	  if (flags.debounce !== undefined) {
   172	    const seconds = Number(flags.debounce);
   173	    if (!Number.isFinite(seconds) || seconds <= 0) return usageError(io, flags, 'checkpoint', '--debounce <seconds> must be a positive number');
   174	    const stampPath = `${paths.logDir}/debounce-${platform}.json`;
   175	    const proceed = withLock(root, io, () => {
   176	      const stamp = safeReadJson(io.fs, stampPath);
   177	      if (stamp.ok && typeof stamp.value?.at === 'string' && Date.parse(io.now()) - Date.parse(stamp.value.at) < seconds * 1000) {
   178	        return false;
   179	      }
   180	      ensureDir(io.fs, paths.logDir);
   181	      atomicWriteJson(io.fs, stampPath, { at: io.now() });
   182	      return true;
   183	    });
   184	    if (!proceed) return finish({ ok: true, data: { events: 0, rewritten: false, debounced: true } }, 0);
   185	  }
   186	
   187	  let active = bundle;
   188	  let mustRewrite = false;
   189	
   190	  // Semantic isolation (plan §Concurrency): a stable foreign session or a
   191	  // mismatched origin platform must not merge into the active bundle. Checked
   192	  // before ANY write so a rejection leaves the whole tree untouched.
   193	  const originHint = active?.origin?.sessionHint;
   194	  const hasStableOwner = active !== null && typeof originHint === 'string' && originHint.length > 0 && active.origin.unstable !== true;
   195	  const stableIncoming = events.find(
   196	    (/** @type {any} */ e) => typeof e.sessionHint === 'string' && e.sessionHint.length > 0 && e.unstable === false,
   197	  );
   198	  const unstableIncoming = events.some((/** @type {any} */ e) => e.unstable === true);
   199	
   200	  if (hasStableOwner && stableIncoming) {
   201	    const foreign = stableIncoming.sessionHint !== originHint || platform !== active.origin.platform;
   202	    if (foreign && flags['take-over'] !== true) {
   203	      io.stderr.write(
   204	        `baton checkpoint: event from a foreign session/origin (${platform}/${stableIncoming.sessionHint} vs active ${active.origin.platform}/${originHint}) — rejected to keep sessions isolated; rerun with --take-over to archive the active bundle and start fresh\n`,
   205	      );
   206	      return finish({ ok: false, error: { code: 'foreign-session', msg: 'event from a foreign session/origin rejected; rerun with --take-over' } }, 0);
   207	    }
   208	    if (foreign) {
   209	      rotateJournal(root, 'takeover', io);
   210	      const fresh = emptyBundle(
   211	        { platform, model: typeof flags.model === 'string' ? flags.model : 'unknown', goal: active.task?.goal ?? 'unknown' },
   212	        io.now(),
   213	      );
   214	      fresh.origin.sessionHint = stableIncoming.sessionHint;
   215	      writeSnapshot(root, fresh, io);
   216	      io.stderr.write(`baton checkpoint: took over — prior bundle archived to history/, fresh bundle owned by ${platform}/${stableIncoming.sessionHint}\n`);
   217	      active = fresh;
   218	      mustRewrite = true;
   219	    }
   220	  }
   221	
   222	  if (unstableIncoming) {
   223	    io.stderr.write('baton checkpoint: event carries an unstable (generated) session hint — ownership unverifiable; applied with this warning only\n');
   224	  }
   225	
   226	  if (active === null) {
   227	    active = emptyBundle(
   228	      { platform, model: typeof flags.model === 'string' ? flags.model : 'unknown', goal: 'unknown' },
   229	      io.now(),
   230	    );
   231	    writeSnapshot(root, active, io);
   232	    // Self-applying seed event (gate-2 iter-2 B6): a journal-only rebuild
   233	    // (snapshot + .bak both lost) must restore the bundle's identity, not the
   234	    // "unknown" placeholder resolveBase seeds. bundle.seed carries bundleId,
   235	    // origin, task, and generation and is applied on replay.
   236	    appendJournal(
   237	      root,
   238	      {
   239	        ts: io.now(),
   240	        type: 'bundle.seed',
   241	        payload: {
   242	          bundleId: active.bundleId,
   243	          generation: active.generation,
   244	          createdAt: active.createdAt,
   245	          origin: active.origin,
   246	          task: { goal: active.task.goal },
   247	          text: `[seed] auto-seeded a new bundle on ${platform} (model ${active.origin.model})`,
   248	        },
   249	        writerId: [platform, io.pid, 'seed'].join('-'),
   250	        dedupeKey: dedupeKey({ ts: io.now(), type: 'bundle.seed', bundleId: active.bundleId }),
   251	      },
   252	      io,
   253	    );
   254	    io.stderr.write('baton checkpoint: no bundle found — auto-seeded a new one at .handoff/bundle.json\n');
   255	    mustRewrite = true;
   256	  }
   257	
   258	  // Ownership adoption (gate-2 fix 2): a bundle whose owner is unverified — a
   259	  // fresh seed (null hint) or a receive that defaulted to an unstable hint —
   260	  // adopts the first STABLE same-platform session as its owner, so semantic
   261	  // isolation engages from then on instead of never.
   262	  const ownerUnverified = active.origin.sessionHint === null || active.origin.unstable === true;
   263	  if (ownerUnverified && stableIncoming && active.origin.platform === platform) {
   264	    active = { ...active, origin: { ...active.origin, sessionHint: stableIncoming.sessionHint, unstable: false } };
   265	    writeSnapshot(root, active, io);
   266	    io.stderr.write(`baton checkpoint: adopted ${platform}/${stableIncoming.sessionHint} as the bundle owner (first verified session)\n`);
   267	    mustRewrite = true;
   268	  }
   269	
   270	  let lastSeq = 0;
   271	  for (const ev of events) {
   272	    const ts = typeof ev.ts === 'string' ? ev.ts : io.now();
   273	    const entry = {
   274	      ...ev,
   275	      ts,
   276	      dedupeKey:
   277	        typeof ev.dedupeKey === 'string' && ev.dedupeKey.length > 0
   278	          ? ev.dedupeKey
   279	          : dedupeKey({ ts, type: ev.type, payload: ev.payload, source: ev.source, sessionHint: ev.sessionHint }),
   280	      writerId: [platform, io.pid, ev.sessionHint ?? 'local'].join('-'),
   281	    };
   282	    lastSeq = appendJournal(root, entry, io);
   283	  }
   284	
   285	  const snap = safeReadJson(io.fs, paths.snapshot);
   286	  const snapSeq = snap.ok && typeof snap.value?.journalSeq === 'number' ? snap.value.journalSeq : 0;
   287	  const snapUpdated = snap.ok && typeof snap.value?.updatedAt === 'string' ? Date.parse(snap.value.updatedAt) : 0;
   288	  const important = events.some((/** @type {any} */ e) => !ROUTINE_TYPES.has(e.type));
   289	  const elapsed = Date.parse(io.now()) - snapUpdated > THROTTLE_WINDOW_MS;
   290	  const accreted = lastSeq - snapSeq;
   291	
   292	  let rewritten = false;
   293	  if (mustRewrite || important || elapsed || accreted >= THROTTLE_EVENT_COUNT) {
   294	    const merged = loadBundle(root, io).bundle;
   295	    if (merged) {
   296	      // Bounded git refresh on EVERY snapshot rewrite (gate-2 iter-2 M1): a
   297	      // mechanical checkpoint is defined to carry branch/HEAD/dirty, so any
   298	      // rewrite — routine Stop that cleared the throttle included, not only
   299	      // "important" events — refreshes git; unavailability degrades to the
   300	      // previous git state (or null), never a failure.
   301	      const git = await gitSnapshot({ execFile: io.execFile, cwd: root, fs: io.fs });
   302	      if (git !== null) {
   303	        merged.git = git;
   304	      } else {
   305	        // git refresh failed/timed out (iter-4 I2/F5): NEVER retain the prior
   306	        // snapshot — stale HEAD/dirty presented as current corrupts the
   307	        // receive-side evidence audit (plan §Root discovery & degraded modes).
   308	        // Clear to an explicit unavailable (null) state and warn; receive
   309	        // re-derives git live. A repo that was never git (git already null)
   310	        // warns nothing — there is no stale state to clear.
   311	        if (merged.git !== null) {
   312	          io.stderr.write('baton checkpoint: git refresh failed — clearing stale git state (marked unavailable; receive re-derives it live)\n');
   313	        }
   314	        merged.git = null;
   315	      }
   316	      writeSnapshot(root, merged, io);
   317	      rewritten = true;
   318	    }
   319	  }
   320	  return finish({ ok: true, data: { events: events.length, rewritten } }, 0);
   321	}
     1	import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
     2	import { join, dirname } from 'node:path';
     3	import { bundlePaths, loadBundle, writeSnapshot, rotateJournal } from '../bundle/store.mjs';
     4	import { snapshot as gitSnapshot } from '../git/snapshot.mjs';
     5	import { loadSignatures } from '../detect/signatures.mjs';
     6	import { classify } from '../detect/classifier.mjs';
     7	import { emitEnvelope, parseFlags, resolveRoot, usageError } from './shared.mjs';
     8	
     9	const BUILTIN_SIGNATURES = join(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..', '..', 'data', 'signatures.v1.json');
    10	
    11	/**
    12	 * Infer the reason class by running the reason text through the classifier
    13	 * against the bundle's origin platform. No signature match reads as 'ok',
    14	 * which is not a switch reason — degrade to null rather than mislabel.
    15	 * @param {string} reason @param {string} platform @param {any} io
    16	 * @returns {string | null}
    17	 */
    18	function inferReasonClass(reason, platform, io) {
    19	  try {
    20	    const table = loadSignatures({ builtinPath: BUILTIN_SIGNATURES }, io);
    21	    const verdict = classify({ text: reason, exitCode: 0, platform, table, structured: null });
    22	    return verdict.class === 'ok' ? null : verdict.class;
    23	  } catch {
    24	    return null;
    25	  }
    26	}
    27	
    28	/**
    29	 * `baton finalize` — the narrative seal: records the switch reason, refreshes
    30	 * git state, seals the bundle, and freezes this generation into history via a
    31	 * 'finalize' journal rotation.
    32	 * @param {string[]} args @param {any} io
    33	 * @returns {Promise<number>}
    34	 */
    35	export async function cmdFinalize(args, io) {
    36	  const { flags } = parseFlags(args);
    37	  const reason = typeof flags.reason === 'string' ? flags.reason : null;
    38	  if (!reason) return usageError(io, flags, 'finalize', '--reason "<why you are switching>" is required');
    39	  const REASON_CLASSES = ['usage-limit', 'auth', 'throttle', 'other-error'];
    40	  if (flags['reason-class'] !== undefined && !REASON_CLASSES.includes(/** @type {string} */ (flags['reason-class']))) {
    41	    return usageError(io, flags, 'finalize', `--reason-class must be one of ${REASON_CLASSES.join('|')}`);
    42	  }
    43	
    44	  const root = resolveRoot(io, flags);
    45	  const paths = bundlePaths(root);
    46	  const { bundle, warnings } = loadBundle(root, io);
    47	  for (const w of warnings) io.stderr.write(`baton finalize: ${w}\n`);
    48	  if (bundle === null) {
    49	    const error = { code: 'no-bundle', msg: `no handoff bundle at ${paths.snapshot}; nothing to finalize` };
    50	    if (flags.json) emitEnvelope(io, { ok: false, error });
    51	    else io.stderr.write(`baton finalize: ${error.msg}\n`);
    52	    return 1;
    53	  }
    54	
    55	  const git = await gitSnapshot({ execFile: io.execFile, cwd: root, fs: io.fs });
    56	  if (git === null) io.stderr.write('baton finalize: git state unavailable — sealing with git marked unavailable (not the stale prior snapshot)\n');
    57	
    58	  const reasonClass =
    59	    typeof flags['reason-class'] === 'string' ? flags['reason-class'] : inferReasonClass(reason, bundle.origin.platform, io);
    60	
    61	  const sealed = {
    62	    ...bundle,
    63	    // NEVER seal the stale prior git snapshot (iter-4 I2 sibling): a receive
    64	    // audits the seal's git against live state, so stale HEAD/dirty would emit
    65	    // false "HEAD moved"/"dirty mismatch" warnings. On refresh failure seal an
    66	    // explicit unavailable (null) git; receive re-derives it live regardless.
    67	    git,
    68	    updatedAt: io.now(),
    69	    handoff: {
    70	      ...bundle.handoff,
    71	      status: 'sealed',
    72	      reason,
    73	      reasonClass,
    74	      toPlatformHint: typeof flags.to === 'string' ? flags.to : null,
    75	      finalizedAt: io.now(),
    76	    },
    77	  };
    78	
    79	  writeSnapshot(root, sealed, io);
    80	  rotateJournal(root, 'finalize', io);
    81	
    82	  if (flags.json) {
    83	    emitEnvelope(io, { ok: true, data: { bundlePath: paths.snapshot, handoffMdPath: paths.handoffMd } });
    84	  } else {
    85	    io.stdout.write(`sealed — reason: ${reason}${reasonClass ? ` (${reasonClass})` : ''}${sealed.handoff.toPlatformHint ? ` — next: ${sealed.handoff.toPlatformHint}` : ''}\n`);
    86	  }
    87	  return 0;
    88	}

    31	describe('checkpoint — bounded git refresh on important checkpoints', () => {

    33	    const io = makeIo({
    34	      files: { '/repo/.handoff/bundle.json': bundleJson() },
    35	      stdin: importantEvent,
    36	      execResults: GIT_OK,
    37	      now: T0,
    38	    });
    39	    assert.equal(await cmdCheckpoint(['--platform', 'claude-code'], io), 0);
    40	    const snap = JSON.parse(io.files()['/repo/.handoff/bundle.json']);
    41	    assert.equal(snap.git?.branch, 'main');
    42	    assert.equal(snap.git?.headSha, 'abc1234def');
    43	    assert.equal(snap.git?.dirty, true);
    44	  });
    45	
    46	  it('git unavailable degrades silently — checkpoint still lands, git stays null', async () => {
    47	    const io = makeIo({ files: { '/repo/.handoff/bundle.json': bundleJson() }, stdin: importantEvent, now: T0 });
    48	    assert.equal(await cmdCheckpoint(['--platform', 'claude-code'], io), 0);
    49	    const snap = JSON.parse(io.files()['/repo/.handoff/bundle.json']);
    50	    assert.equal(snap.git, null);
    51	    assert.ok(snap.decisions.some((/** @type {any} */ d) => d.summary === 'chose approach A'));
    52	  });
    53	
    54	  it('git refresh FAILURE clears a prior git section, never presents stale (iter-4 I2/F5)', async () => {
    55	    // A bundle that already carries a git snapshot, then a checkpoint where git
    56	    // is unavailable (no execResults). The stale HEAD/dirty must NOT survive —
    57	    // receive audits git claims against live state, so stale-as-current is a bug.
    58	    const withGit = JSON.parse(bundleJson());
    59	    withGit.git = { branch: 'main', headSha: 'OLDSHA', dirty: false, dirtySummary: [], contentDigest: 'old', summaryTruncated: false };
    60	    const io = makeIo({ files: { '/repo/.handoff/bundle.json': JSON.stringify(withGit, null, 2) + '\n' }, stdin: importantEvent, now: T0 });
    61	    assert.equal(await cmdCheckpoint(['--platform', 'claude-code'], io), 0);
    62	    const snap = JSON.parse(io.files()['/repo/.handoff/bundle.json']);
    63	    assert.equal(snap.git, null, 'stale git is cleared to explicit unavailable, not retained');
    64	    assert.match(io.stderrText(), /git refresh failed|git.*unavailable|stale git/i, 'a degradation warning is surfaced');
    65	  });
    66	});
    67	
    68	describe('checkpoint — config-gated transcript tail capture (plan §Transcript policy)', () => {
    69	  const messages = [];
    70	  for (let i = 1; i <= 14; i++) messages.push(JSON.stringify({ role: i % 2 ? 'user' : 'assistant', content: `message-${i}` }));
    71	  messages.push(JSON.stringify({ role: 'assistant', content: 'the key is sk-ant-api03-PLANTEDSECRET123456 ok' }));
    72	  const transcript = messages.join('\n') + '\n';
    73	  const preCompact = JSON.stringify({ hook_event_name: 'PreCompact', session_id: 's1', transcript_path: '/repo/.claude/t.jsonl' });
    74	
    75	  const files = (config) => ({
    76	    '/repo/.handoff/bundle.json': bundleJson(),
    77	    '/repo/.claude/t.jsonl': transcript,
    78	    ...(config === null ? {} : { '/repo/baton.config.json': JSON.stringify(config) }),
    79	  });
    80	
    81	  it('OFF by default: no transcript field even at PreCompact with a path', async () => {
    82	    const io = makeIo({ files: files(null), stdin: preCompact, now: T0 });
    83	    assert.equal(await cmdCheckpoint(['--platform', 'claude-code'], io), 0);
    84	    const snap = JSON.parse(io.files()['/repo/.handoff/bundle.json']);
    85	    assert.equal(snap.transcript, undefined);
    86	    assert.doesNotMatch(io.files()['/repo/.handoff/journal.ndjson'] ?? '', /message-1/);
    87	  });
    88	
    89	  it('ON: captures the bounded, redacted tail at PreCompact; HANDOFF.md never shows it', async () => {
    90	    const io = makeIo({
    91	      files: files({ schema: 'baton/config@1', capture: { transcriptTail: true } }),
    92	      stdin: preCompact,
    93	      now: T0,
    94	    });
    95	    assert.equal(await cmdCheckpoint(['--platform', 'claude-code'], io), 0);
    96	    const snap = JSON.parse(io.files()['/repo/.handoff/bundle.json']);
    97	    assert.equal(typeof snap.transcript?.tail, 'string');
    98	    assert.match(snap.transcript.tail, /message-14/, 'recent messages are in the tail');
    99	    assert.doesNotMatch(snap.transcript.tail, /message-1\b/, 'only the LAST 10 messages are kept');
   100	    assert.ok(Buffer.byteLength(snap.transcript.tail) <= 8192, '8 KB cap enforced');
   101	    assert.doesNotMatch(snap.transcript.tail, /PLANTEDSECRET/, 'planted secret is redacted');
   102	    assert.match(snap.transcript.tail, /\[redacted\]/);
   103	    assert.doesNotMatch(io.files()['/repo/.handoff/HANDOFF.md'], /message-14|\[redacted\]/, 'the tail is excluded from HANDOFF.md');
   104	  });
   105	
   106	  // iter-3 F9: the transcript allowlist compares realpathSync outputs. On
   107	  // Windows those are backslash-spelled, so the caller must go through the
   108	  // separator-normalized containment — a raw startsWith would refuse every
   109	  // in-tree transcript. These pin the cmdCheckpoint CALLER (not just the helper).
   110	  const winRealpath = (io, map) => {
   111	    io.platform = 'win32'; // treat backslashes as separators (iter-4 I9)
   112	    io.fs.realpathSync = (/** @type {any} */ p) => (map[String(p)] ?? String(p));
   113	  };
   114	
   115	  it('ON with Windows backslash realpaths: an in-tree transcript IS captured (F9)', async () => {
   116	    const io = makeIo({
   117	      files: files({ schema: 'baton/config@1', capture: { transcriptTail: true } }),
   118	      stdin: preCompact,
   119	      now: T0,
   120	    });
   121	    // Simulate Windows realpaths for the root, the managed tree, and the
   122	    // transcript file (.handoff must resolve consistently or the checkpoint's
   123	    // own jail refuses the write before capture is even reached).
   124	    winRealpath(io, { '/repo': 'C:\\repo', '/repo/.handoff': 'C:\\repo\\.handoff', '/repo/.claude/t.jsonl': 'C:\\repo\\.claude\\t.jsonl' });
   125	    assert.equal(await cmdCheckpoint(['--platform', 'claude-code'], io), 0);
   126	    const snap = JSON.parse(io.files()['/repo/.handoff/bundle.json']);
   127	    assert.equal(typeof snap.transcript?.tail, 'string', 'an in-tree transcript is captured despite backslash realpaths');
   128	  });
   129	
   130	  it('ON but the transcript realpath escapes to another volume: NOT captured (F9)', async () => {
   131	    const io = makeIo({
   132	      files: files({ schema: 'baton/config@1', capture: { transcriptTail: true } }),
   133	      stdin: preCompact,
   134	      now: T0,
   135	    });
   136	    // The transcript path resolves outside the repo (a different volume); the
   137	    // managed tree still resolves in-repo so the checkpoint itself can write.
   138	    winRealpath(io, { '/repo': 'C:\\repo', '/repo/.handoff': 'C:\\repo\\.handoff', '/repo/.claude/t.jsonl': 'D:\\evil\\t.jsonl' });
   139	    assert.equal(await cmdCheckpoint(['--platform', 'claude-code'], io), 0);
   140	    const snap = JSON.parse(io.files()['/repo/.handoff/bundle.json']);
   141	    assert.equal(snap.transcript, undefined, 'a cross-volume transcript is refused by the containment check');
   142	  });
   143	
   144	  it('ON but the transcript realpath contains a backslash on POSIX: NOT captured (iter-4 I9)', async () => {
   145	    const io = makeIo({
   146	      files: files({ schema: 'baton/config@1', capture: { transcriptTail: true } }),
   147	      stdin: preCompact,
   148	      now: T0,
   149	    });
   150	    io.platform = 'linux';
   151	    // A backslash is a legal posix filename char; treating it as a separator
   152	    // would let '/repo\\evil/t.jsonl' normalize under the repo root.
   153	    io.fs.realpathSync = (/** @type {any} */ p) => (String(p) === '/repo/.claude/t.jsonl' ? '/repo\\evil/t.jsonl' : String(p));
   154	    assert.equal(await cmdCheckpoint(['--platform', 'claude-code'], io), 0);
   155	    const snap = JSON.parse(io.files()['/repo/.handoff/bundle.json']);
   156	    assert.equal(snap.transcript, undefined, 'a backslash-bearing transcript realpath is refused off Windows');
   157	  });
   158	
   159	  it('ON but a routine Stop event (not PreCompact): no capture', async () => {
   160	    const io = makeIo({
   161	      files: files({ schema: 'baton/config@1', capture: { transcriptTail: true } }),
   162	      stdin: JSON.stringify({ hook_event_name: 'Stop', session_id: 's1', transcript_path: '/repo/.claude/t.jsonl' }),
   163	      now: T0,
   164	    });
   165	    assert.equal(await cmdCheckpoint(['--platform', 'claude-code'], io), 0);
   166	    const snapText = io.files()['/repo/.handoff/bundle.json'];
   167	    assert.doesNotMatch(snapText, /message-14/);
   168	  });
   169	});
   170	
   171	describe('writeSnapshot — compaction runs in the snapshot write path', () => {
   172	  it('an oversized decision log is truncated with the compaction marker on disk', () => {
   173	    const b = emptyBundle({ platform: 'claude-code', model: 'm', goal: 'g' }, T0);
   174	    for (let i = 1; i <= 250; i++) b.decisions.push({ seq: i, ts: T0, summary: `decision-${i}` });
   175	    b.journalSeq = 250;
   176	    const io = makeIo({ files: { '/repo/x.txt': 'x' }, now: T0 });
   177	    writeSnapshot('/repo', b, io);
   178	    const snap = JSON.parse(io.files()['/repo/.handoff/bundle.json']);
   179	    assert.ok(snap.decisions.length < 250, `decisions were compacted (got ${snap.decisions.length})`);
   180	    assert.ok(snap.decisions.some((/** @type {any} */ d) => /compacted:/.test(d.summary)), 'the compaction marker is present');
   181	    assert.ok(snap.compaction.droppedDecisions > 0);
   182	    assert.ok(snap.decisions.some((/** @type {any} */ d) => d.summary === 'decision-250'), 'the newest decisions survive');
   183	    assert.ok(snap.decisions.some((/** @type {any} */ d) => d.summary === 'decision-1'), 'the earliest decisions survive');
   184	  });
   185	});
     1	import { describe, it } from 'node:test';
     2	import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
     3	import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
     4	import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
     5	import { join, dirname } from 'node:path';
     6	import { makeIo } from '../helpers/fakeio.mjs';
     7	import { cmdFinalize } from '../../core/src/commands/finalize.mjs';
     8	import { bundlePaths } from '../../core/src/bundle/store.mjs';
     9	import { readAllTolerant } from '../../core/src/util/jsonl.mjs';
    10	
    11	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    12	// Command-level contract for core/src/commands/finalize.mjs. Direct import over
    13	// fakeio: cmdFinalize(args, io) -> exit code (this file `await`s it; finalize
    14	// refreshes git via the async injected execFile, so it is async).
    15	//
    16	// Source of truth: docs/design/core.md §CLI contract (exit codes: 2 usage error);
    17	// plan §Checkpoint engine ("finalize --reason \"…\" [--reason-class] [--to] records
    18	// the switch reason (class inferred from text via the classifier when omitted),
    19	// refreshes git state, freezes history"); §"Journal rotation & crash recovery"
    20	// (finalize rotates the journal, kind 'finalize'); §Bundle handoff block
    21	// (status/reason/reasonClass/toPlatformHint/finalizedAt).
    22	//
    23	// PINS:
    24	//   1. --reason is REQUIRED: absent -> exit 2 (usage error), a message naming it.
    25	//   2. On success (exit 0), the sealed snapshot carries handoff = { status:
    26	//      'sealed', reason: <--reason>, reasonClass: <inferred|--reason-class>,
    27	//      toPlatformHint: <--to | null>, finalizedAt: io.now() }.
    28	//   3. reasonClass INFERENCE: when --reason-class is absent, finalize classifies
    29	//      the reason text through the built-in signature table against the bundle's
    30	//      ORIGIN platform; a usage-limit reason string infers 'usage-limit'. An
    31	//      explicit --reason-class overrides inference (no classification).
    32	//   4. GIT REFRESH: finalize recomputes git via git/snapshot.mjs over the injected
    33	//      execFile; the sealed bundle's `git` reflects the execResults fixtures.
    34	//   5. JOURNAL ROTATION (verifier fold F9 — the full store.rotateJournal contract
    35	//      exercised THROUGH finalize, not just freeze existence): the sealed
    36	//      snapshot is written BEFORE the rotation, so the history/<stem>.finalize.json
    37	//      freeze IS the sealed state; the pre-seal journal is renamed to
    38	//      history/<stem>.finalize.ndjson alongside it (same stem, no entries lost);
    39	//      no rotation.marker.json remains; a fresh EMPTY live journal exists; and
    40	//      per-kind retention prunes finalize rotations to the newest 10.
    41	//   6. --json envelope carries data.bundlePath and data.handoffMdPath (absolute
    42	//      paths under <root>/.handoff/).
    43	//
    44	// loadSignatures reads via io.fs, so tests that exercise inference seed the real
    45	// signature table at its module-resolved path.
    46	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    47	
    48	const here = dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url));
    49	const repoRoot = join(here, '..', '..');
    50	const SIG_PATH = join(repoRoot, 'core', 'data', 'signatures.v1.json');
    51	const SIG_CONTENT = readFileSync(SIG_PATH, 'utf8');
    52	
    53	const NOW = '2026-07-11T12:00:00.000Z';
    54	const paths = bundlePaths('/repo');
    55	
    56	// A clean-repo git command fixture set (see git-snapshot.test.mjs for the keys).
    57	const GIT_CLEAN = {
    58	  'git rev-parse --abbrev-ref HEAD': { stdout: 'main\n' },
    59	  'git rev-parse HEAD': { stdout: 'a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8c9d0\n' },
    60	  'git status --porcelain': { stdout: '' },
    61	  'git diff --cached': { stdout: '' },
    62	  'git diff': { stdout: '' },
    63	  'git ls-files --others --exclude-standard': { stdout: '' },
    64	};
    65	
    66	function mkBundle(overrides = {}) {
    67	  return {
    68	    schema: 'baton/bundle@1',
    69	    bundleId: 'b_final000000000',
    70	    generation: 1,
    71	    createdAt: '2026-07-11T00:00:00.000Z',
    72	    updatedAt: '2026-07-11T00:00:00.000Z',
    73	    origin: { platform: 'claude-code', model: 'claude-fable-5', sessionHint: 'sess-1', unstable: false },
    74	    task: { goal: 'Ship failover v1', constraints: [], acceptance: [] },
    75	    plan: { steps: [] },
    76	    decisions: [],
    77	    files: { touched: [] },
    78	    roles: { assignments: {} },
    79	    git: null,
    80	    handoff: { status: 'open', reason: null, reasonClass: null, toPlatformHint: null, finalizedAt: null, receive_log: [] },
    81	    journalSeq: 0,
    82	    compaction: { droppedDecisions: 0, note: null },
    83	    dedupeRing: [],
    84	    ...overrides,
    85	  };
    86	}
    87	
    88	const snapText = (b) => JSON.stringify(b, null, 2) + '\n';
    89	
    90	function seedIo({ bundle = mkBundle(), execResults = GIT_CLEAN, now = NOW } = {}) {
    91	  return makeIo({
    92	    files: { [SIG_PATH]: SIG_CONTENT, [paths.snapshot]: snapText(bundle) },
    93	    execResults,
    94	    now,
    95	  });
    96	}
    97	
    98	const readSnapshot = (io) => JSON.parse(io.files()[paths.snapshot]);
    99	
   100	// ===========================================================================
   101	describe('finalize — usage validation', () => {
   102	  it('missing --reason -> exit 2 with a message naming the flag', async () => {
   103	    const io = seedIo();
   104	    const code = await cmdFinalize([], io);
   105	    assert.equal(code, 2, 'a missing required flag is a usage error (exit 2)');
   106	    assert.match(io.stderrText(), /reason/i, 'the usage error names --reason');
   107	  });
   108	});
   109	
   110	// ===========================================================================
   111	describe('finalize — seals the bundle', () => {
   112	  it('sets the sealed handoff block, infers reasonClass, records toPlatformHint + finalizedAt; exit 0', async () => {
   113	    const io = seedIo();
   114	    const reason = "You've hit your session limit · resets 3pm";
   115	
   116	    const code = await cmdFinalize(['--reason', reason, '--to', 'codex'], io);
   117	    assert.equal(code, 0);
   118	
   119	    const snap = readSnapshot(io);
   120	    assert.equal(snap.handoff.status, 'sealed');
   121	    assert.equal(snap.handoff.reason, reason);
   122	    assert.equal(snap.handoff.reasonClass, 'usage-limit', 'a usage-limit reason infers reasonClass usage-limit (via the classifier, origin platform)');
   123	    assert.equal(snap.handoff.toPlatformHint, 'codex', 'toPlatformHint comes from --to');
   124	    assert.equal(snap.handoff.finalizedAt, NOW, 'finalizedAt is stamped with io.now()');
   125	  });
   126	
   127	  it('an explicit --reason-class overrides inference', async () => {
   128	    const io = seedIo();
   129	    const code = await cmdFinalize(['--reason', 'switching for a fresh context', '--reason-class', 'throttle', '--to', 'codex'], io);
   130	    assert.equal(code, 0);
   131	    assert.equal(readSnapshot(io).handoff.reasonClass, 'throttle', '--reason-class is used verbatim, no classification');
   132	  });
   133	
   134	  it('refreshes git state from execFile fixtures into the sealed bundle', async () => {
   135	    const io = seedIo();
   136	    await cmdFinalize(['--reason', "You've hit your session limit", '--to', 'codex'], io);
   137	    const snap = readSnapshot(io);
   138	    assert.ok(snap.git, 'finalize populates the git section');
   139	    assert.equal(snap.git.branch, 'main');
   140	    assert.equal(snap.git.headSha, 'a1b2c3d4e5f6a7b8c9d0');
   141	    assert.equal(snap.git.dirty, false);
   142	  });
   143	
   144	  it('(iter-4 I2 sibling) git-refresh FAILURE seals git=null, never the stale prior snapshot', async () => {
   145	    // A seal must not carry a stale HEAD/dirty as current — the receive audits
   146	    // the seal's git against live state and would emit false "HEAD moved"
   147	    // warnings. On refresh failure finalize clears git to explicit unavailable.
   148	    const bundle = mkBundle();
   149	    bundle.git = { branch: 'main', headSha: 'OLDSHA', dirty: false, dirtySummary: [], contentDigest: 'old', summaryTruncated: false };
   150	    const io = seedIo({ bundle, execResults: {} }); // no git fixtures → gitSnapshot returns null
   151	    const code = await cmdFinalize(['--reason', "You've hit your session limit", '--to', 'codex'], io);
   152	    assert.equal(code, 0);
   153	    assert.equal(readSnapshot(io).git, null, 'stale git is cleared, not sealed as current');
   154	    assert.match(io.stderrText(), /git.*unavailable/i, 'a git-unavailable warning is surfaced');
   155	  });
   156	
   157	  it('(F9) rotates the journal kind "finalize": sealed freeze + journal pair, marker cleared, live journal emptied', async () => {
   158	    const io = seedIo();
   159	    // A pending journal entry that must ride into the rotated history journal.
   160	    io.fs.writeFileSync(
   161	      paths.journal,
   162	      JSON.stringify({ seq: 1, ts: '2026-07-11T00:30:00.000Z', type: 'note', dedupeKey: 'pre', source: 'stop', payload: { text: 'pre-seal note' } }) + '\n',
   163	    );
   164	
   165	    const code = await cmdFinalize(['--reason', "You've hit your session limit", '--to', 'codex'], io);
   166	    assert.equal(code, 0);
   167	
   168	    const names = io.fs.readdirSync(paths.historyDir);
   169	    const freezeName = names.find((n) => /\.finalize\.json$/.test(n));
   170	    assert.ok(freezeName, 'a finalize freeze exists in history/');
   171	    const stem = freezeName.slice(0, -'.json'.length);
   172	    assert.ok(names.includes(`${stem}.ndjson`), 'the rotated journal lands alongside the freeze (same stem)');
   173	
   174	    const freeze = JSON.parse(io.files()[`${paths.historyDir}/${freezeName}`]);
   175	    assert.equal(freeze.handoff.status, 'sealed', 'the frozen snapshot is the SEALED state — the seal is written before the rotation');
   176	
   177	    const rotated = readAllTolerant(io.fs, `${paths.historyDir}/${stem}.ndjson`);
   178	    assert.ok(rotated.entries.some((e) => e.payload?.text === 'pre-seal note'), 'the pre-seal journal entries ride into history — nothing is lost');
   179	
   180	    assert.equal(io.fs.existsSync(`${paths.dir}/rotation.marker.json`), false, 'the rotation marker is cleared on success');
   181	    assert.ok(io.fs.existsSync(paths.journal), 'a fresh live journal exists');
   182	    assert.equal(readAllTolerant(io.fs, paths.journal).entries.length, 0, 'the fresh live journal is empty');
   183	
   184	    // (iter-2) Crash-safe ORDER, not just the final state: marker → freeze +
   185	    // rotated journal → fresh live journal → marker cleared, proven from the
   186	    // memfs mutation history (each entry is a full post-mutation snapshot).
   187	    const markerPath = `${paths.dir}/rotation.marker.json`;
   188	    const states = io.fs.__history.map((h) => h.files);
   189	    const firstIndex = (pred) => states.findIndex(pred);
   190	    const iMarker = firstIndex((f) => markerPath in f);
   191	    const iFreeze = firstIndex((f) => Object.keys(f).some((k) => k.startsWith(`${paths.historyDir}/`) && k.endsWith('.finalize.json')));
   192	    const iRotated = firstIndex((f) => Object.keys(f).some((k) => k.startsWith(`${paths.historyDir}/`) && k.endsWith('.finalize.ndjson')));
   193	    assert.ok(iMarker !== -1, 'the rotation marker was written at some point (marker-backed rotation, not a lucky final state)');
   194	    assert.ok(iFreeze !== -1 && iMarker < iFreeze, 'the marker becomes visible BEFORE the history freeze lands');
   195	    assert.ok(iRotated !== -1 && iMarker < iRotated, 'the marker becomes visible BEFORE the journal is rotated into history');
   196	    const iFresh = states.findIndex((f, idx) => idx > iRotated && paths.journal in f);
   197	    assert.ok(iFresh !== -1, 'a fresh live journal is created after the rotation');
   198	    const iCleared = states.findIndex((f, idx) => idx > iMarker && !(markerPath in f));
   199	    assert.ok(iCleared !== -1, 'the marker is eventually removed');
   200	    assert.ok(
   201	      iCleared > iFreeze && iCleared > iRotated && iCleared > iFresh,
   202	      'the marker is removed LAST — after freeze, rotated journal, and fresh live journal all exist',
   203	    );
   204	  });
   205	
   206	  it('(F9) retains only the newest 10 finalize rotations (per-kind pruning through the command)', async () => {
   207	    const io = seedIo();
   208	    for (let i = 0; i < 11; i += 1) {
   209	      io.setNow(`2026-07-11T13:${String(i).padStart(2, '0')}:00.000Z`); // distinct stems per rotation
   210	      // Re-seed an open bundle so each iteration has something to seal.
   211	      io.fs.writeFileSync(paths.snapshot, snapText(mkBundle()));
   212	      const code = await cmdFinalize(['--reason', "You've hit your session limit", '--to', 'codex'], io);
   213	      assert.equal(code, 0);
   214	    }
   215	    const names = io.fs.readdirSync(paths.historyDir);
   216	    assert.equal(names.filter((n) => /\.finalize\.json$/.test(n)).length, 10, 'exactly 10 finalize freezes retained after 11 finalizes');
   217	    assert.equal(names.filter((n) => /\.finalize\.ndjson$/.test(n)).length, 10, 'exactly 10 finalize journals retained');
   218	  });
   219	});
   220	
   221	// ===========================================================================
   222	describe('finalize — --json envelope', () => {
   223	  it('emits one envelope whose data carries bundlePath + handoffMdPath', async () => {
   224	    const io = seedIo();
   225	    const code = await cmdFinalize(['--reason', "You've hit your session limit", '--to', 'codex', '--json'], io);
   226	    assert.equal(code, 0);
   227	
   228	    const parsed = JSON.parse(io.stdoutText());
   229	    assert.equal(io.stdoutText(), JSON.stringify(parsed) + '\n', 'stdout is exactly one compact envelope + newline');
   230	    assert.equal(parsed.ok, true);

     1	import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
     2	import { join, dirname } from 'node:path';
     3	import { loadBundle, writeSnapshotIn, rotateJournalIn } from '../bundle/store.mjs';
     4	import { withLock } from '../bundle/lock.mjs';
     5	import { loadConfig } from '../roles/matrix.mjs';
     6	import { resolveRoles } from '../roles/resolve.mjs';
     7	import { renderResumePrompt } from './prompt.mjs';
     8	import { loadSignatures } from '../detect/signatures.mjs';
     9	import { classify } from '../detect/classifier.mjs';
    10	import { dedupeKey } from '../util/ids.mjs';
    11	
    12	const BUILTIN_SIGNATURES = join(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..', '..', 'data', 'signatures.v1.json');
    13	const STALE_AFTER_MS = 12 * 60 * 60 * 1000;
    14	
    15	/** @typedef {{platform: string, origin: string, reason: string, gitSnapshot: any, probes?: any, sessionHint: string, sessionUnstable?: boolean, reasonClass?: string}} ReceiveOpts */
    16	
    17	/**
    18	 * Classify the switch reason, KEEPING confidence (gate-2 iter-2 M4): a
    19	 * low-confidence heuristic (e.g. Cursor's "quota exceeded") must not silently
    20	 * drive an avoid[] decision — the caller warns and defers to explicit intake.
    21	 * @param {string} reason @param {string} originPlatform @param {any} io
    22	 * @returns {{class: string | null, confidence: string | null}}
    23	 */
    24	function classifyReason(reason, originPlatform, io) {
    25	  try {
    26	    const table = loadSignatures({ builtinPath: BUILTIN_SIGNATURES }, io);
    27	    const verdict = classify({ text: reason, exitCode: 0, platform: originPlatform, table, structured: null });
    28	    return verdict.class === 'ok' ? { class: null, confidence: null } : { class: verdict.class, confidence: verdict.confidence };
    29	  } catch {
    30	    return { class: null, confidence: null };
    31	  }
    32	}
    33	
    34	/**
    35	 * The revision fingerprint the receipt token is a hash of. Every field is a
    36	 * bound input: any drift between prepare and commit re-derives a different
    37	 * token, so a stale receipt can never commit (plan §Concurrency).
    38	 * @param {string} root @param {any} bundle @param {ReceiveOpts} opts @param {any} io
    39	 */
    40	function deriveToken(root, bundle, opts, io) {
    41	  let configDigest = 'absent';
    42	  try {
    43	    configDigest = dedupeKey(io.fs.readFileSync(`${root}/baton.config.json`, 'utf8'));
    44	  } catch {
    45	    // no config — 'absent' is itself a bound value
    46	  }
    47	  return dedupeKey({
    48	    generation: bundle.generation,
    49	    journalSeq: bundle.journalSeq,
    50	    // The seal state is a bound input too (gate-2 fix): a competing receiver
    51	    // that lands between this token's derivation and its commit changes
    52	    // status/receive_log, so the loser's token cannot re-derive.
    53	    handoffStatus: bundle.handoff?.status ?? null,
    54	    receives: Array.isArray(bundle.handoff?.receive_log) ? bundle.handoff.receive_log.length : 0,
    55	    origin: opts.origin,
    56	    reason: opts.reason,
    57	    // Explicit --reason-class is a bound intake input (gate-2 iter-2 M4): it
    58	    // changes the avoid[] decision, so a change between prepare and commit is
    59	    // drift that must re-prepare.
    60	    reasonClass: opts.reasonClass ?? null,
    61	    configDigest,
    62	    gitDigest: dedupeKey(opts.gitSnapshot ?? null),
    63	    probesDigest: dedupeKey(opts.probes ?? null),
    64	    platform: opts.platform,
    65	    sessionHint: opts.sessionHint,
    66	  });
    67	}
    68	
    69	/**
    70	 * @param {any} bundle @param {ReceiveOpts} opts @param {any} io
    71	 * @returns {string[]} staleness/degradation warnings
    72	 */
    73	function stalenessWarnings(bundle, opts, io) {
    74	  /** @type {string[]} */
    75	  const warnings = [];
    76	  if (bundle.handoff.status === 'open') {
    77	    warnings.push('source bundle is unsealed (open) — the handoff was never finalized; treating this as a degraded (limit-death) receive');
    78	  }
    79	  const ref = bundle.handoff.finalizedAt ?? bundle.updatedAt;
    80	  const ageMs = Date.parse(io.now()) - Date.parse(ref);
    81	  if (Number.isFinite(ageMs) && ageMs > STALE_AFTER_MS) {
    82	    warnings.push(`bundle finalized ${Math.round(ageMs / 3_600_000)}h ago (staleness threshold 12h) — re-verify before continuing`);
    83	  }
    84	  const g = opts.gitSnapshot;
    85	  if (g && bundle.git) {
    86	    if (bundle.git.headSha !== g.headSha) {
    87	      warnings.push(`HEAD moved since capture: bundle recorded ${bundle.git.headSha}, working tree now ${g.headSha}`);
    88	    }
    89	    if (bundle.git.dirty !== g.dirty) {
    90	      warnings.push(`dirty-state mismatch: bundle captured dirty=${bundle.git.dirty}, working tree now dirty=${g.dirty}`);
    91	    }
    92	  }
    93	  return warnings;
    94	}
    95	
    96	/**
    97	 * Phase 1 of the receive transaction: validate, resolve roles, render the
    98	 * resume prompt, and return a one-time receipt token. Mutates NOTHING — the
    99	 * token is self-contained (a fingerprint hash), never a persisted record.
   100	 * @param {string} root @param {ReceiveOpts} opts @param {any} io
   101	 * @returns {{token: string, prompt: string, assignments: Record<string, any>, warnings: string[]}}
   102	 */
   103	export function prepare(root, opts, io) {
   104	  const { bundle, warnings: loadWarnings } = loadBundle(root, io);
   105	  if (bundle === null) throw new Error(`no handoff bundle under ${root}/.handoff — nothing to receive`);
   106	
   107	  const warnings = [...loadWarnings, ...stalenessWarnings(bundle, opts, io)];
   108	
   109	  const { config, errors } = loadConfig(root, io);
   110	  /** @type {Record<string, any>} */
   111	  let assignments = {};
   112	  if (config) {
   113	    // The dead origin comes from the BUNDLE first (its sealed reasonClass and
   114	    // recorded origin platform); typed intake only fills the gaps — a user
   115	    // should not need to retype the verbatim limit string to keep roles off
   116	    // the platform that died (gate-2 reviewer-b finding 4). Confidence gates
   117	    // auto-avoidance (gate-2 iter-2 M4): an explicit --reason-class or a sealed
   118	    // reasonClass is trusted; a live text classification only auto-avoids at
   119	    // medium+ confidence, else it warns and defers to explicit intake.
   120	    let reasonClass;
   121	    let confidence;
   122	    if (typeof opts.reasonClass === 'string') {
   123	      reasonClass = opts.reasonClass;
   124	      confidence = 'high'; // explicit user intake
   125	    } else if (typeof bundle.handoff?.reasonClass === 'string') {
   126	      reasonClass = bundle.handoff.reasonClass;
   127	      confidence = 'high'; // trusted seal
   128	    } else {
   129	      const v = classifyReason(opts.reason, opts.origin, io);
   130	      reasonClass = v.class;
   131	      confidence = v.confidence;
   132	    }
   133	    const deadOrigin =
   134	      typeof bundle.origin?.platform === 'string' && bundle.origin.platform !== 'unknown' ? bundle.origin.platform : opts.origin;
   135	    /** @type {string[]} */
   136	    let avoid = [];
   137	    if (reasonClass === 'usage-limit') {
   138	      if (confidence === 'low') {
   139	        warnings.push(
   140	          `the switch reason matched a usage-limit heuristic only at LOW confidence — not auto-avoiding ${deadOrigin}; pass --reason-class usage-limit to confirm the failover away from it`,
   141	        );
   142	      } else {
   143	        avoid = [deadOrigin];
   144	      }
   145	    }
   146	    assignments = resolveRoles({ config, to: opts.platform, avoid, nativeOnly: false, probes: opts.probes ?? null }).assignments;
   147	  } else {
   148	    warnings.push(`role matrix unavailable (${errors.map((e) => e.msg).join('; ')}) — no role table in this prompt`);
   149	  }
   150	
   151	  const prompt = renderResumePrompt({ bundle, assignments, warnings, origin: opts.origin, reason: opts.reason });
   152	  return { token: deriveToken(root, bundle, opts, io), prompt, assignments, warnings };
   153	}
   154	
   155	/**
   156	 * Phase 2: one atomic transition. Re-derives every bound input from current
   157	 * disk state + opts and rejects ANY drift (throws, zero mutation). On match:
   158	 * writes the received seal, archives it via a 'receive' rotation, then opens a
   159	 * fresh writable generation owned by the receiving platform/session.
   160	 * @param {string} root @param {string} token @param {ReceiveOpts} opts @param {any} io
   161	 * @returns {{adopted: boolean, generation: number, archivedTo: string}}
   162	 */
   163	export function commit(root, token, opts, io) {
   164	  // ONE operation-scoped lock across revalidation and every mutation (gate-2
   165	  // fix: reviewers a#1/b#11): two competing receivers serialize here, and the
   166	  // loser's re-derivation sees the winner's generation/status/receive_log.
   167	  return withLock(root, io, (fence) => commitLocked(root, token, opts, io, fence));
   168	}
   169	
   170	/**
   171	 * @param {string} root @param {string} token @param {ReceiveOpts} opts @param {any} io @param {string} fence
   172	 * @returns {{adopted: boolean, generation: number, archivedTo: string}}
   173	 */
   174	function commitLocked(root, token, opts, io, fence) {
   175	  const { bundle } = loadBundle(root, io);
   176	  if (bundle === null) throw new Error(`no handoff bundle under ${root}/.handoff — nothing to receive; run receive --prepare first`);
   177	
   178	  if (deriveToken(root, bundle, opts, io) !== token) {
   179	    throw new Error(
   180	      'receipt token is stale: a bound input (bundle revision, intake, config, git state, probes, or target platform/session) drifted since prepare — run re-prepare and retry',
   181	    );
   182	  }
   183	
   184	  const degraded = bundle.handoff.status !== 'sealed';
   185	  const receiveEntry = {
   186	    origin: opts.origin,
   187	    reason: opts.reason,
   188	    at: io.now(),
   189	    ...(degraded ? { degradedSeal: true } : {}),
   190	  };
   191	
   192	  // The degraded seal must NOT persist a LOW-confidence classification (iter-3
   193	  // F10): a sealed reasonClass is trusted as high-confidence by the next
   194	  // prepare, so laundering a low-confidence heuristic here would silently
   195	  // auto-avoid the origin one hop later. Explicit intake is high-confidence and
   196	  // seals; a live classification seals ONLY at medium+ confidence, else null
   197	  // (the next receive re-classifies the reason text at its true confidence).
   198	  let sealedReasonClass = null;
   199	  if (degraded) {
   200	    if (typeof opts.reasonClass === 'string') {
   201	      sealedReasonClass = opts.reasonClass;
   202	    } else {
   203	      const v = classifyReason(opts.reason, opts.origin, io);
   204	      sealedReasonClass = v.confidence === 'low' ? null : v.class;
   205	    }
   206	  }
   207	
   208	  const receivedSeal = {
   209	    ...bundle,
   210	    updatedAt: io.now(),
   211	    handoff: {
   212	      ...bundle.handoff,
   213	      status: 'received',
   214	      ...(degraded
   215	        ? {
   216	            reason: opts.reason,
   217	            reasonClass: sealedReasonClass,
   218	            finalizedAt: io.now(),
   219	          }
   220	        : {}),
   221	      receive_log: [...bundle.handoff.receive_log, receiveEntry],
   222	    },
   223	  };
   224	
   225	  // Freeze order: the received seal is written first so the 'receive' rotation
   226	  // archives exactly that state (never the pre-commit bundle). All three
   227	  // mutations run under the SAME lock acquisition, fence-checked per write.
   228	  writeSnapshotIn(root, receivedSeal, io, fence);
   229	  const archivedTo = rotateJournalIn(root, 'receive', io, fence);
   230	
   231	  const { config } = loadConfig(root, io);
   232	  const next = {
   233	    ...receivedSeal,
   234	    generation: bundle.generation + 1,
   235	    origin: {
   236	      platform: opts.platform,
   237	      model: config?.defaults?.[opts.platform] ?? 'unknown',
   238	      sessionHint: opts.sessionHint,
   239	      unstable: opts.sessionUnstable === true,
   240	    },
   241	    // The fresh writable generation has NO switch reason yet (iter-4 I3): the
   242	    // sealed reason/reasonClass/finalizedAt/toPlatformHint describe the PRIOR
   243	    // handoff, not this one. Carrying them forward made the next prepare trust a
   244	    // stale class as a high-confidence seal (silently auto-avoiding the healthy
   245	    // adopted origin) and poisoned both the 12 h staleness reference and the
   246	    // sessionStart limit-hit signal. Keep only receive_log (the audit chain).
   247	    handoff: { ...receivedSeal.handoff, status: 'open', reason: null, reasonClass: null, finalizedAt: null, toPlatformHint: null },
   248	  };
   249	  writeSnapshotIn(root, next, io, fence);
   250	
   251	  return { adopted: true, generation: next.generation, archivedTo };
   252	}
49-//   names re-prepare (matches /re-?prepare/i) and performs NO mutation (the
50-//   .handoff tree is byte-identical before and after the rejected commit). Because
51-//   a successful commit bumps `generation`, an already-spent token (double-commit)
52-//   and a losing competitor's token (competing receivers) both fail the revision
53-//   check with no extra bookkeeping.
54-//
55-// COMMIT SUCCESS (one atomic transition):
56-//   - Archives the current bundle to history/ via rotateJournal kind 'receive'
57-//     (a history/<stem>.receive.json + .ndjson pair). archivedTo === that stem.
58-//     The archived .receive freeze is the RECEIVED seal (handoff.status
59-//     'received' — asserted on BOTH the sealed and open paths, verifier fold F7:
60-//     archiving the pre-commit sealed bundle unchanged would be wrong); it
61-//     retains the receipt data (the receive_log entry for THIS receive rides in
62-//     the archived freeze). For an OPEN source bundle it is the receive-generated
63://     DEGRADED seal (handoff.reason from intake, reasonClass classified from the
64-//     reason text against the intake ORIGIN platform via the built-in signature
65://     table); for a SEALED source the finalize reason/reasonClass are retained.
66-//   - Adopts ownership: origin becomes the RECEIVING side — origin.platform ===
67-//     opts.platform, origin.sessionHint === opts.sessionHint.
68://   - Opens a fresh writable generation: generation + 1, handoff.status 'open'.
69-//   - Appends a receive_log entry { origin, reason, at }; for the OPEN (degraded)
70-//     path the entry additionally carries degradedSeal: true (PIN: that flag lives
71-//     on the receive_log entry). The sealed path's entry has NO truthy degradedSeal.
72-//   - Returns { adopted: <truthy>, generation: <new gen>, archivedTo: <stem> }.
73-//

 succeeded in 0ms:
     1	import { probeRegexSafe } from './probe.mjs';
     2	
     3	const PLATFORMS = ['claude-code', 'codex', 'cursor'];
     4	const CLASSES = ['usage-limit', 'auth', 'throttle', 'other-error'];
     5	const MATCHER_KINDS = ['substring', 'regex', 'json-field'];
     6	const REGEX_MAX_LEN = 200;
     7	
     8	/**
     9	 * Is a quantified group's body ambiguous? Alternation, another quantifier, or
    10	 * any nested group inside a `(…)+`/`(…)*`/`(…){` makes backtracking
    11	 * super-linear in the worst case — conservatively rejected (gate-2 fix 6:
    12	 * the old lint accepted `(a|aa)+$` and the `(a|a)*x` live repro).
    13	 * @param {string} body
    14	 */
    15	function riskyGroupBody(body) {
    16	  // Strip a non-capture/lookaround/named marker so `(?:abc)+` reads as `abc`.
    17	  const b = body.replace(/^\?(?:<=|<!|:|=|!|<[^>]*>)/, '');
    18	  let inClass = false;
    19	  for (let i = 0; i < b.length; i++) {
    20	    const c = b[i];
    21	    if (c === '\\') {
    22	      i++;
    23	      continue;
    24	    }
    25	    if (inClass) {
    26	      if (c === ']') inClass = false;
    27	      continue;
    28	    }
    29	    if (c === '[') {
    30	      inClass = true;
    31	      continue;
    32	    }
    33	    if (c === '|' || c === '+' || c === '*' || c === '{' || c === '?' || c === '(' || c === ')') return true;
    34	  }
    35	  return false;
    36	}
    37	
    38	/**
    39	 * Structural scan: walk the pattern (class- and escape-aware), and for every
    40	 * group followed by an unbounded quantifier check its full body — any nesting
    41	 * depth — for ambiguity. Returns a problem string or null.
    42	 * @param {string} pattern
    43	 */
    44	function scanQuantifiedGroups(pattern) {
    45	  /** @type {number[]} */
    46	  const opens = [];
    47	  let inClass = false;
    48	  for (let i = 0; i < pattern.length; i++) {
    49	    const c = pattern[i];
    50	    if (c === '\\') {
    51	      i++;
    52	      continue;
    53	    }
    54	    if (inClass) {
    55	      if (c === ']') inClass = false;
    56	      continue;
    57	    }
    58	    if (c === '[') {
    59	      inClass = true;
    60	      continue;
    61	    }
    62	    if (c === '(') {
    63	      opens.push(i);
    64	      continue;
    65	    }
    66	    if (c === ')') {
    67	      const start = opens.pop();
    68	      if (start === undefined) continue; // unbalanced — new RegExp() reports it
    69	      const next = pattern[i + 1];
    70	      if (next === '+' || next === '*' || next === '{') {
    71	        const body = pattern.slice(start + 1, i);
    72	        if (riskyGroupBody(body)) {
    73	          return `quantified group "(${body})" contains alternation, another quantifier, or a nested group`;
    74	        }
    75	      }
    76	    }
    77	  }
    78	  return null;
    79	}
    80	
    81	/**
    82	 * Approximate character-set overlap between two simple-atom alphabets.
    83	 * Descriptors: {all:true} (dot / broad negated shorthand) | {sh:'d'|'w'|'s'} |
    84	 * {lit:Set<string>}. Returns true unless the two sets are PROVABLY disjoint —
    85	 * conservative for untrusted overlays (iter-4 I4).
    86	 * @param {any} a @param {any} b
    87	 */
    88	function alphasOverlap(a, b) {
    89	  if (!a || !b) return false;
    90	  if (a.all || b.all) return true;
    91	  if (a.sh && b.sh) {
    92	    if (a.sh === b.sh) return true;
    93	    // \s is disjoint from \d and \w; \d ⊂ \w so they overlap.
    94	    return !(a.sh === 's' || b.sh === 's');
    95	  }
    96	  if (a.lit && b.lit) {
    97	    for (const c of a.lit) if (b.lit.has(c)) return true;
    98	    return false;
    99	  }
   100	  const sh = a.sh ? a.sh : b.sh;
   101	  const lit = a.lit ? a.lit : b.lit;
   102	  for (const c of lit) {
   103	    if (sh === 'd' && c >= '0' && c <= '9') return true;
   104	    if (sh === 'w' && /[A-Za-z0-9_]/.test(c)) return true;
   105	    if (sh === 's' && /\s/.test(c)) return true;
   106	  }
   107	  return false;
   108	}
   109	
   110	/**
   111	 * Structural scan for a run of ≥2 CONSECUTIVE atoms, each with an UNBOUNDED
   112	 * quantifier (`*`, `+`, `{n,}`), whose alphabets overlap — `.*.*…`, `\d*\d*…`,
   113	 * `\(*\(*…`, `\n*\n*…`, `[a-z]*[a-z]*…`. Such a chain partitions the same input
   114	 * combinatorially and backtracks super-linearly, yet is invisible to the
   115	 * quantified-GROUP scan; and because a finite probe alphabet cannot guarantee a
   116	 * non-matching tail for every atom (`.` accepts almost everything), rejecting
   117	 * the STRUCTURE at load is the reliable guard (iter-4 I4, reviewer-a #7 /
   118	 * reviewer-b #3). Groups reset the run (the group scan covers quantified groups;
   119	 * a group's overlap with a simple atom is not decided here). Returns a problem
   120	 * string or null.
   121	 * @param {string} pattern
   122	 * @returns {string | null}
   123	 */
   124	function scanChainedQuantifiedAtoms(pattern) {
   125	  const p = pattern;
   126	  /** @type {any} */
   127	  let prev = null; // alpha of the previous atom IF it carried an unbounded quantifier, else null
   128	
   129	  for (let i = 0; i < p.length; ) {
   130	    const c = p[i];
   131	    // Chain-breakers: alternation and anchors end any run.
   132	    if (c === '|' || c === '^' || c === '$') {
   133	      prev = null;
   134	      i += 1;
   135	      continue;
   136	    }
   137	    /** @type {any} */
   138	    let alpha = null;
   139	    let isGroup = false;
   140	    if (c === '\\') {
   141	      const n = p[i + 1];
   142	      // decode length of the escape token
   143	      let len = 2;
   144	      if (n === 'x') len = 4;
   145	      else if (n === 'u') len = p[i + 2] === '{' ? (p.indexOf('}', i) - i + 1) : 6;
   146	      if (n === 'd' || n === 'w' || n === 's') alpha = { sh: n };
   147	      else if (n === 'D' || n === 'W' || n === 'S' || n === 'b' || n === 'B') alpha = { all: true }; // broad/negated or anchor-ish → conservative
   148	      else alpha = { lit: new Set([decodeEscape(p.slice(i, i + len))]) }; // \(, \n, \t, \xNN, \uNNNN → the literal char
   149	      i += Math.max(2, len);
   150	    } else if (c === '[') {
   151	      const end = classEnd(p, i);
   152	      const body = p.slice(i + 1, end);
   153	      alpha = body.startsWith('^') ? { all: true } : classAlpha(body);
   154	      i = end + 1;
   155	    } else if (c === '(') {
   156	      // Skip the whole group (balance-aware); recurse into its body for chains.
   157	      const end = groupEnd(p, i);
   158	      const inner = scanChainedQuantifiedAtoms(p.slice(i + 1, end));
   159	      if (inner) return inner;
   160	      isGroup = true;
   161	      i = end + 1;
   162	    } else if (c === '.') {
   163	      alpha = { all: true };
   164	      i += 1;
   165	    } else {
   166	      alpha = { lit: new Set([c]) };
   167	      i += 1;
   168	    }
   169	
   170	    // Read an optional quantifier and classify it as unbounded or not.
   171	    let unbounded = false;
   172	    const q = p[i];
   173	    if (q === '*' || q === '+') {
   174	      unbounded = true;
   175	      i += 1;
   176	    } else if (q === '{') {
   177	      const close = p.indexOf('}', i);
   178	      if (close !== -1) {
   179	        const spec = p.slice(i + 1, close);
   180	        // {n,} (no upper bound) is unbounded; {n} and {n,m} are bounded.
   181	        unbounded = /^\d+,\s*$/.test(spec);
   182	        i = close + 1;
   183	      }
   184	    } else if (q === '?') {
   185	      i += 1;
   186	    }
   187	    // consume a lazy/possessive modifier if present
   188	    if (p[i] === '?' || p[i] === '+') i += 1;
   189	
   190	    if (isGroup) {
   191	      // A group breaks the simple-atom run.
   192	      prev = null;
   193	      continue;
   194	    }
   195	    // prev + current = a run of ≥2 CONSECUTIVE overlapping unbounded-quantified
   196	    // atoms → super-linear backtracking. Reject.
   197	    if (unbounded && prev && alphasOverlap(prev, alpha)) {
   198	      return 'chained overlapping unbounded quantifiers (e.g. `.*.*`, `\\d*\\d*`, `\\(*\\(*`) backtrack super-linearly';
   199	    }
   200	    prev = unbounded ? alpha : null;
   201	  }
   202	  return null;
   203	}
   204	
   205	/** Decode a single-char escape token (`\(`, `\n`, `\t`, `\xNN`, `\uNNNN`, `\u{...}`) to its literal char. @param {string} tok */
   206	function decodeEscape(tok) {
   207	  const rest = tok.slice(1);
   208	  if (rest[0] === 'x') return String.fromCharCode(parseInt(rest.slice(1, 3), 16));
   209	  if (rest[0] === 'u') {
   210	    const h = rest[1] === '{' ? rest.slice(2, rest.indexOf('}')) : rest.slice(1, 5);
   211	    try {
   212	      return String.fromCodePoint(parseInt(h, 16));
   213	    } catch {
   214	      return rest[0];
   215	    }
   216	  }
   217	  const named = { n: '\n', t: '\t', r: '\r', f: '\f', v: '\v', 0: '\0' };
   218	  return named[/** @type {'n'} */ (rest[0])] ?? rest[0];
   219	}
   220	
   221	/** Index of the `]` closing a class opened at `open` (a `]` immediately after `[`/`[^` is a literal). @param {string} p @param {number} open */
   222	function classEnd(p, open) {
   223	  let i = open + 1;
   224	  if (p[i] === '^') i += 1;
   225	  if (p[i] === ']') i += 1; // leading ] is literal
   226	  for (; i < p.length; i++) {
   227	    if (p[i] === '\\') {
   228	      i += 1;
   229	      continue;
   230	    }
   231	    if (p[i] === ']') return i;
   232	  }
   233	  return p.length - 1;
   234	}
   235	
   236	/** Index of the `)` closing a group opened at `open` (balance-aware, class/escape-safe). @param {string} p @param {number} open */
   237	function groupEnd(p, open) {
   238	  let depth = 0;
   239	  let inClass = false;
   240	  for (let i = open; i < p.length; i++) {
   241	    const c = p[i];
   242	    if (c === '\\') {
   243	      i += 1;
   244	      continue;
   245	    }
   246	    if (inClass) {
   247	      if (c === ']') inClass = false;
   248	      continue;
   249	    }
   250	    if (c === '[') inClass = true;
   251	    else if (c === '(') depth += 1;
   252	    else if (c === ')') {
   253	      depth -= 1;
   254	      if (depth === 0) return i;
   255	    }
   256	  }
   257	  return p.length - 1;
   258	}
   259	
   260	/** Approximate alphabet of a (non-negated) class body: literals + range endpoints + shorthands. @param {string} body */
   261	function classAlpha(body) {
   262	  if (/\\[dwsDWS]/.test(body) || /\\[DWS]/.test(body)) return { all: true }; // shorthand inside a class → treat broadly
   263	  /** @type {Set<string>} */
   264	  const lit = new Set();
   265	  for (let i = 0; i < body.length; i++) {
   266	    if (body[i] === '\\') {
   267	      lit.add(decodeEscape(body.slice(i, i + 2)));
   268	      i += 1;
   269	      continue;
   270	    }
   271	    // range a-z: add endpoints (a run of an endpoint char backtracks the same)
   272	    if (body[i + 1] === '-' && body[i + 2] !== undefined) {
   273	      lit.add(body[i]);
   274	      lit.add(body[i + 2]);
   275	      i += 2;
   276	      continue;
   277	    }
   278	    lit.add(body[i]);
   279	  }
   280	  return { lit };
   281	}
   282	
   283	const ALLOWED_FLAGS = 'dgimsuvy';
   284	
   285	/**
   286	 * Validate a regex flags string (gate-2 iter-2 B3): every char must be an
   287	 * allowed JS flag with no duplicates, so a flags value that compiles nowhere
   288	 * (`z`, `ii`) is rejected at LOAD, not left to throw later inside classify().
   289	 * @param {string} id @param {string} flags
   290	 */
   291	function lintFlags(id, flags) {
   292	  if (typeof flags !== 'string') throw new Error(`signature ${id}: regex flags must be a string`);
   293	  const seen = new Set();
   294	  for (const c of flags) {
   295	    if (!ALLOWED_FLAGS.includes(c)) throw new Error(`signature ${id}: unsupported regex flag "${c}" (allowed: ${ALLOWED_FLAGS})`);
   296	    if (seen.has(c)) throw new Error(`signature ${id}: duplicate regex flag "${c}"`);
   297	    seen.add(c);
   298	  }
   299	}
   300	
   301	/**
   302	 * Lint one regex pattern together with its flags. With the load-time worker
   303	 * probe on overlay tables (see probe.mjs), the runtime time-guard is by
   304	 * construction: patterns that could backtrack catastrophically never reach
   305	 * classify(), which stays synchronous with no timers.
   306	 * @param {string} id @param {string} pattern @param {string} [flags]
   307	 */
   308	function lintRegex(id, pattern, flags = '') {
   309	  if (typeof pattern !== 'string' || pattern.length > REGEX_MAX_LEN) {
   310	    throw new Error(`signature ${id}: regex pattern exceeds the ${REGEX_MAX_LEN}-char length cap (or is not a string)`);
   311	  }
   312	  if (/\\[1-9]/.test(pattern)) {
   313	    throw new Error(`signature ${id}: regex backreferences are not allowed`);
   314	  }
   315	  lintFlags(id, flags);
   316	  const problem = scanQuantifiedGroups(pattern) || scanChainedQuantifiedAtoms(pattern);
   317	  if (problem) {
   318	    throw new Error(`signature ${id}: ${problem} (pathological backtracking / non-linear-time risk)`);
   319	  }
   320	  try {
   321	    // eslint-disable-next-line no-new
   322	    new RegExp(pattern, flags);
   323	  } catch (err) {
   324	    throw new Error(`signature ${id}: invalid regex pattern/flags (${/** @type {any} */ (err)?.message ?? 'syntax error'})`);
   325	  }
   326	}
   327	
   328	/** @param {any} s */
   329	function lintSignature(s) {
   330	  if (typeof s.id !== 'string' || s.id.length === 0) throw new Error('signature with missing id');
   331	  if (!PLATFORMS.includes(s.platform)) {
   332	    throw new Error(`signature ${s.id}: unknown platform "${s.platform}" (expected ${PLATFORMS.join('|')})`);
   333	  }
   334	  if (!CLASSES.includes(s.class)) throw new Error(`signature ${s.id}: unknown class "${s.class}"`);
   335	  if (!s.matcher || !MATCHER_KINDS.includes(s.matcher.kind)) {
   336	    throw new Error(`signature ${s.id}: unknown matcher kind "${s.matcher?.kind}" (expected ${MATCHER_KINDS.join('|')})`);
   337	  }
   338	  if (s.matcher.kind === 'regex') lintRegex(s.id, s.matcher.pattern, s.matcher.flags ?? '');
   339	  if (s.matcher.kind === 'substring' && typeof s.matcher.value !== 'string') {
   340	    throw new Error(`signature ${s.id}: substring matcher needs a string value`);
   341	  }
   342	  if (s.matcher.kind === 'json-field' && typeof s.matcher.path !== 'string') {
   343	    throw new Error(`signature ${s.id}: json-field matcher needs a dotted string path`);
   344	  }
   345	  if (s.resetHint !== undefined) lintRegex(s.id, s.resetHint);
   346	}
   347	
   348	/** @param {any} fs @param {string} path @param {{probe?: boolean}} [opts] */
   349	function readTable(fs, path, opts = {}) {
   350	  const raw = fs.readFileSync(path, 'utf8');
   351	  const table = JSON.parse(raw);
   352	  if (table?.schema !== 'baton/signatures@1') {
   353	    throw new Error(`signature table at ${path}: expected schema baton/signatures@1, got ${JSON.stringify(table?.schema)}`);
   354	  }
   355	  if (!Array.isArray(table.signatures)) throw new Error(`signature table at ${path}: signatures must be an array`);
   356	  for (const s of table.signatures) {
   357	    lintSignature(s);
   358	    // Overlay tables are untrusted: the structural lint cannot see group-free
   359	    // pathological shapes (chained stars), so every overlay regex additionally
   360	    // runs against adversarial inputs in a worker with a hard deadline.
   361	    if (opts.probe === true) {
   362	      for (const pattern of [s.matcher?.kind === 'regex' ? s.matcher.pattern : null, s.resetHint ?? null]) {
   363	        if (typeof pattern !== 'string') continue;
   364	        const verdict = probeRegexSafe(pattern, s.matcher?.flags ?? '');
   365	        if (!verdict.safe) throw new Error(`signature ${s.id}: ${verdict.reason}`);
   366	      }
   367	    }
   368	  }
   369	  return table;
   370	}
   371	
   372	/**
   373	 * Load + validate the signature table, merging an optional overlay by id
   374	 * (later wins — the drift-absorbing hot-patch surface). Builtin patterns are
   375	 * package-shipped and statically linted; overlay patterns are additionally
   376	 * probe-guarded at load.
   377	 * @param {{builtinPath: string, overlayPath?: string}} paths @param {any} io
   378	 */
   379	export function loadSignatures({ builtinPath, overlayPath }, io) {
   380	  const base = readTable(io.fs, builtinPath);
   381	  if (!overlayPath) return base;
   382	
   383	  const overlay = readTable(io.fs, overlayPath, { probe: true });
   384	  const merged = [...base.signatures];
   385	  for (const s of overlay.signatures) {
   386	    const i = merged.findIndex((b) => b.id === s.id);
   387	    if (i === -1) merged.push(s);
   388	    else merged[i] = s;
   389	  }
   390	  return { ...base, updated: overlay.updated ?? base.updated, signatures: merged };
     1	import { describe, it } from 'node:test';
     2	import assert from 'node:assert/strict';
     3	import { fileURLToPath } from 'node:url';
     4	import { join, dirname } from 'node:path';
     5	import { readFileSync } from 'node:fs';
     6	import { makeIo } from '../helpers/fakeio.mjs';
     7	import { loadSignatures } from '../../core/src/detect/signatures.mjs';
     8	import { probeRegexSafe } from '../../core/src/detect/probe.mjs';
     9	
    10	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    11	// Gate-2 iteration-2 findings B3 (reviewer-a #5) + M8 (reviewer-b #1):
    12	// - B3: lint must validate the pattern WITH its flags (an unsupported/duplicate
    13	//   flag that compiles nowhere must be rejected at load, not throw later in
    14	//   classify), and the worker probe must distinguish a compile error from a
    15	//   timeout.
    16	// - M8: the probe's adversarial inputs were built only from literal
    17	//   [A-Za-z0-9] in the pattern, so a group-free chained-star over \d/\s/[!-/]
    18	//   passed the scanner AND the probe, then hung classify. Probe inputs must be
    19	//   generated per character-class so such a pattern is caught.
    20	// ---------------------------------------------------------------------------
    21	
    22	const BUILTIN = join(dirname(fileURLToPath(import.meta.url)), '..', '..', 'core', 'data', 'signatures.v1.json');
    23	
    24	function overlay(sig) {
    25	  return JSON.stringify({ schema: 'baton/signatures@1', signatures: [{ id: 'ov', platform: 'codex', class: 'usage-limit', confidence: 'medium', ...sig }] });
    26	}
    27	function loadWith(sig) {
    28	  const io = makeIo({ files: { '/overlay.json': overlay(sig), '/builtin.json': readFileSync(BUILTIN, 'utf8') } });
    29	  return loadSignatures({ builtinPath: '/builtin.json', overlayPath: '/overlay.json' }, io);
    30	}
